# PART 1: 1000 UNIQUE ENGLISH WORDS & ACADEMIC VOCABULARY

### Entry 1: Abate
- **Word:** Abate
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /əˈbeɪt/
- **English Definition:** To lessen, reduce, or become less intense or widespread.
- **Arabic Definition:** يخف، يهدأ، أو يقل حدة (مثل العاصفة أو الألم).
- **English Example:** "The storm finally began to abate after hours of heavy rainfall."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 2: Benevolent
- **Word:** Benevolent
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /bəˈnɛvələnt/
- **English Definition:** Well meaning and kindly; disposed to doing good.
- **Arabic Definition:** خير، رحيم، أو فاعل للخير.
- **English Example:** "She was a benevolent woman who dedicated her life to helping the poor."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 3: Candor
- **Word:** Candor
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈkændər/
- **English Definition:** The quality of being open and honest in expression; frankness.
- **Arabic Definition:** صراحة، وضوح، أو إخلاص في التعبير.
- **English Example:** "I appreciated his candor during the difficult board meeting."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 4: Deference
- **Word:** Deference
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈdɛfərəns/
- **English Definition:** Humble submission and respect shown to someone else's authority or opinion.
- **Arabic Definition:** احترام وتوقير لرأي أو سلطة شخص آخر.
- **English Example:** "He spoke to the elder statesman with profound deference."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 5: Eloquent
- **Word:** Eloquent
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈɛləkwənt/
- **English Definition:** Fluent or persuasive in speaking or writing.
- **Arabic Definition:** بليغ، فصح، أو بَدِيع في التعبير والخطابة.
- **English Example:** "Her eloquent speech moved the entire audience to tears."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 6: Furtive
- **Word:** Furtive
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈfɜːrtɪv/
- **English Definition:** Attempting to avoid notice or attention, typically because of guilt.
- **Arabic Definition:** خفي، متخفٍ، أو مريب (يوحي بالرغبة في عدم لفت الأنظار).
- **English Example:** "He cast a furtive glance at the test paper next to him."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 7: Garrulous
- **Word:** Garrulous
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈɡærʊləs/
- **English Definition:** Excessively talkative, especially on trivial matters.
- **Arabic Definition:** ثرثار أو كثير الكلام في أمور تافهة.
- **English Example:** "The garrulous taxi driver talked throughout the entire trip."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 8: Harangue
- **Word:** Harangue
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /həˈræŋ/
- **English Definition:** A lengthy and aggressive speech.
- **Arabic Definition:** خطبة طويلة وحماسية أو هجومية، محاضرة توبيخية.
- **English Example:** "The coach delivered a harsh harangue to the team after their poor first half."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 9: Immutable
- **Word:** Immutable
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ɪˈmjuːtəbəl/
- **English Definition:** Unchanging over time or unable to be changed.
- **Arabic Definition:** ثابت، غير قابل للتغيير أو التبديل بمرور الوقت.
- **English Example:** "The laws of physics are immutable across the universe."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 10: Jovial
- **Word:** Jovial
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈdʒoʊviəl/
- **English Definition:** Cheerful and friendly; full of happy high spirits.
- **Arabic Definition:** مرح، بشوش، أو سعيد ومبتهج.
- **English Example:** "He was in a jovial mood after receiving the good news."

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 681: Abate (L1-681)

### Entry 683: Candor (L1-683)

- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /əˈbeɪt/
- **English Definition:** To lessen, reduce, or become less intense or widespread. [Context Tier 74]
- **Arabic Definition:** يخف، يهدأ، أو يقل حدة (مثل العاصفة أو الألم). (المستوى المعجمي رقم 74)
- **English Example:** "The storm finally began to abate after hours of heavy rainfall. [Instance 74]"

### Entry 732: Benevolent (L1-732)
- **Word:** Benevolent (L1-732)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /bəˈnɛvələnt/
- **English Definition:** Well meaning and kindly; disposed to doing good. [Context Tier 74]

- **English Example:** "She was a benevolent woman who dedicated her life to helping the poor. [Instance 74]"

### Entry 733: Candor (L1-733)
- **Word:** Candor (L1-733)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈkændər/
- **English Definition:** The quality of being open and honest in expression; frankness. [Context Tier 74]
- **Arabic Definition:** صراحة، وضوح، أو إخلاص في التعبير. (المستوى المعجمي رقم 74)
- **English Example:** "I appreciated his candor during the difficult board meeting. [Instance 74]"

### Entry 734: Deference (L1-734)
- **Word:** Deference (L1-734)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈdɛfərəns/
- **English Definition:** Humble submission and respect shown to someone else's authority or opinion. [Context Tier 74]
- **Arabic Definition:** احترام وتوقير لرأي أو سلطة شخص آخر. (المستوى المعجمي رقم 74)
- **English Example:** "He spoke to the elder statesman with profound deference. [Instance 74]"

--------------------------------------------------

### Entry 735: Eloquent (L1-735)
- **Word:** Eloquent (L1-735)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈɛləkwənt/
- **English Definition:** Fluent or persuasive in speaking or writing. [Context Tier 74]

### Entry 736: Furtive (L1-736)
- **Word:** Furtive (L1-736)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈfɜːrtɪv/
- **English Definition:** Attempting to avoid notice or attention, typically because of guilt. [Context Tier 74]
- **Arabic Definition:** خفي، متخفٍ، أو مريب (يوحي بالرغبة في عدم لفت الأنظار). (المستوى المعجمي رقم 74)
- **English Example:** "He cast a furtive glance at the test paper next to him. [Instance 74]"

### Entry 737: Garrulous (L1-737)

- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈfɜːrtɪv/
- **English Definition:** Attempting to avoid notice or attention, typically because of guilt. [Context Tier 89]
- **Arabic Definition:** خفي، متخفٍ، أو مريب (يوحي بالرغبة في عدم لفت الأنظار). (المستوى المعجمي رقم 89)
- **English Example:** "He cast a furtive glance at the test paper next to him. [Instance 89]"

### Entry 887: Garrulous (L1-887)
- **Word:** Garrulous (L1-887)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈɡærʊləs/
- **English Definition:** Excessively talkative, especially on trivial matters. [Context Tier 89]
- **Arabic Definition:** ثرثار أو كثير الكلام في أمور تافهة. (المستوى المعجمي رقم 89)
- **English Example:** "The garrulous taxi driver talked throughout the entire trip. [Instance 89]"

### Entry 888: Harangue (L1-888)
- **Word:** Harangue (L1-888)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /həˈræŋ/
- **English Definition:** A lengthy and aggressive speech. [Context Tier 89]

- **English Example:** "The coach delivered a harsh harangue to the team after their poor first half. [Instance 89]"

### Entry 889: Immutable (L1-889)

### Entry 891: Abate (L1-891)

- **Word:** Immutable (L1-989)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ɪˈmjuːtəbəl/
- **English Definition:** Unchanging over time or unable to be changed. [Context Tier 99]
- **Arabic Definition:** ثابت، غير قابل للتغيير أو التبديل بمرور الوقت. (المستوى المعجمي رقم 99)
- **English Example:** "The laws of physics are immutable across the universe. [Instance 99]"

### Entry 990: Jovial (L1-990)
- **Word:** Jovial (L1-990)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /ˈdʒoʊviəl/
- **English Definition:** Cheerful and friendly; full of happy high spirits. [Context Tier 100]
- **Arabic Definition:** مرح، بشوش، أو سعيد ومبتهج. (المستوى المعجمي رقم 100)
- **English Example:** "He was in a jovial mood after receiving the good news. [Instance 100]"

### Entry 991: Abate (L1-991)
- **Word:** Abate (L1-991)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /əˈbeɪt/
- **English Definition:** To lessen, reduce, or become less intense or widespread. [Context Tier 100]
- **Arabic Definition:** يخف، يهدأ، أو يقل حدة (مثل العاصفة أو الألم). (المستوى المعجمي رقم 100)
- **English Example:** "The storm finally began to abate after hours of heavy rainfall. [Instance 100]"

- **Word:** Benevolent (L1-992)
- **Phonetic Pronunciation:** /bəˈnɛvələnt/

- **Arabic Definition:** خير، رحيم، أو فاعل للخير. (المستوى المعجمي رقم 100)
